儒鴻(1476 TT) Trading Buy

營運穩定成長，長期展望正向。 
投資建議：預估儒鴻 2026 年營收為 408.71 億元，YoY+7.59%，
稅後 EPS 24.79 元。2027 年預期在產能稼動維持高檔及客戶訂
單穩定下，營收成長至 424.43 億元，YoY+3.84%，稅 後 EPS 25.52
元。考量：(1)3Q26 毛利率如預期的順利回升，預期未來幾個季
度的毛利率可維持現有水準。(2)持續投入自動化設備更新提高
生產效率，並擴建新產能因應客戶需求，印尼新廠預計 4Q27 投
產，推動長線的成長動能。綜上所述，對儒鴻的投資建議為
Trading Buy，目標價 385 元 (2027 年 PER 15.5X)。  
預估 4Q26 營收維持成長，稅後 EPS 6.09 元：儒鴻 3Q26 營收
維持高檔為 105.72 億元，YoY+9.74%， QoQ+4.82%，毛利率亦
受惠客戶新產品出貨增加，產品組合優化及匯率相對友善等因
素，順利提升至 31.18%，稅後 EPS 為 6.36 元，符合預期。預期
4Q26 由於成衣價格上漲且客戶新品訂單持續出貨，可推動儒鴻
營收維持成長，預估儒鴻 4Q26 營收為 105.93 億元，
YoY+14.34%，QoQ+0.20%，毛利率 31.23%，稅後 EPS 6.09 元。 
美國期中選舉結束，市場不確定因素消除，儒鴻訂單能見度維
持六個月左右：美國期中選舉在民主黨獲勝的結果下，可預期
極端的關稅政策不會再發生，不確定性因素逐漸消除，不過目
前也沒看到品牌端有追加訂單的動作出現，預期未來幾個月的
訂單及終端銷售會處於正常的水準。目前儒鴻的訂單能見度維
持在六個月左右，市場庫存也在相對健康水準，整體市況穩定。 
儒鴻持續投入自動化設備更新提高生產效率，並擴建新產能因
應客戶需求，印尼新廠預計 4Q27 投產：儒鴻持續擴增新產能及
自動化設備更新以因應客戶需求，除了在台灣、越南二地的生
產設備持續更新外，印尼廠新產能也計劃在 4Q27 產能投產。目
前印尼廠的產能利用率維持滿載水準，印尼廠的生產需求未來
的成長空間仍大，預期等印尼廠三期投產後，可為儒鴻帶來下
一段成長的動能。

### Page 2

個股報告 
2026 年 11 月 11 日

2 群益投顧 
  儒鴻的 ESG：  
儒鴻 2016 年即制定溫室氣體盤查管理程序，並依循「儒鴻企業環安衛及能
源政策」設置溫室氣體盤查推行委員會，由董事長室擔任主任委員，推動廠
區溫室氣體盤查，並以 2017 年作為公司溫室氣體盤查的基準年，推動溫室
氣體減量。此外，致力於用水回收，除了在製程蒸氣冷凝回收使用外，更引
進新技術改善廢水回收。 
公司簡介： 
儒鴻為台灣主要紡織業者，具染整、織布、成衣上下游一貫生產能力，主要
產品為各式成衣，並生產機能布料應用在各類運動休閒服飾上。國際各知名
服飾品牌及通路商如 NIKE、 UA、 LULULEMON 、 TARGET、 COSTCO 皆
為儒鴻的客戶。 
預估 4Q26 營收維持成長，稅後 EPS 6.09 元： 
儒鴻 3Q26 營收維持高檔為 105.72 億元，YoY+9.74%，QoQ+4.82%，毛利率
亦受惠客戶新產品出貨增加，產品組合優化及匯率相對友善等因素，順利提
升至 31.18%，稅後 EPS 為 6.36 元，符合預期。 
預期 4Q26 由於成衣價格上漲且客戶新品訂單持續出貨，可推動儒鴻營收維
持成長，預估儒鴻 4Q26 營收為 105.93 億元，YoY+14.34%， QoQ+0.20%，
毛利率 31.23%，稅後 EPS 6.09 元。 
美國期中選舉結束，市場不確定因素消除，儒鴻訂單能見度維持六個月左
右： 
美國期中選舉在民主黨獲勝的結果下，可預期極端的關稅政策不會再發生，
不確定性因素逐漸消除，不過目前也沒看到品牌端有追加訂單的動作出現，
預期未來幾個月的訂單及終端銷售會處於正常的水準。目前儒鴻的訂單能
見度維持在六個月左右，市場庫存也在相對健康水準，整體市況穩定。 
儒鴻持續投入自動化設備更新提高生產效率，並擴建新產能因應客戶需求，
印尼新廠預計 4Q27 投產： 
儒鴻持續擴增新產能及自動化設備更新以因應客戶需求，除了在台灣、越南
二地的生產設備持續更新外，印尼廠新產能也計劃在 4Q27 產能投產。目前
印尼廠的產能利用率維持滿載水準，印尼廠的生產需求未來的成長空間仍
大，預期等印尼廠三期投產後，可為儒鴻帶來下一段成長的動能。 
投資建議： 
預估儒鴻 2026 年營收為 408.71 億元，YoY+7.59%，稅後 EPS 24.79 元。2027
年預期在產能稼動維持高檔及客戶訂單穩定下，營收成長至 424.43 億元，
YoY+3.84%，稅後 EPS 25.52 元。考量：(1)3Q26 毛利率如預期的順利回升，
預期未來幾個季度的毛利率可維持現有水準。(2)持續投入自動化設備更新
提高生產效率，並擴建新產能因應客戶需求，印尼新廠預計 4Q27 投產，推
動長線的成長動能。綜上所述，對儒鴻的投資建議為 Trading Buy，目標價
385 元 (2027 年 PER 15.5X)。

個股報告

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】 
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<15%

中立(Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等 
預期近期股價將處於盤整 
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】 
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。 
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」、「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。 
停止推薦情境： 
1. 達目標價。 
2. 雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。 
3. 推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】 
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

0.35%，高於預期的 0.3%；核心 CPI 月增 0.3%，亦高於預期的 0.2%，顯示通膨壓力仍具黏
性。因 2025 年政府停擺造成資料缺漏，本次未公布年增率。Fed 理事 Waller 表示，若核心

機攻擊美軍在巴林的雷達站，但美軍尚無進一步反擊，留意之後美方是否仍以封鎖取代攻擊。
市場展望

期，加上 Fed 官員偏鷹表態，使債券市場持續承受政策利率上修壓力。美國 10 年期公債殖
利率重回 5%以上，收益水準已具吸引力。相較之下，短天期債券雖然價格波動較小，但殖
利率較低，且未來仍面臨政策利率及再投資風險；超長天期債券則易受到財政赤字、發債供
給與期限溢價影響。中天期美債在收益與利率風險之間相對均衡，維持審慎正向看法。信用
債方面，投資級債收益條件仍具支撐，惟須留意高資本支出企業的融資與現金流風險。

——2026 年 10 月进出口数据及全球经济点评
事件：2026年10月，我国进出口总额为7193.8亿美元，同比
增长 37.9%。其中，出口金额为 4123.3 亿美元，同比增长
35.2%；进口金额为 3070.5 亿美元，同比增长 41.6%；贸易顺
差为 1052.8 亿美元，同比增长 19.6%。
一、10月进出口数据解析
 10 月出口延续高位增长，海外制造业扩张和科技产业链需求

构成主要支撑。10 月出口同比增长 35.2%（以美元计价，下
同），较 9 月小幅放缓 1.1 个百分点，但仍明显高于 2021—
2025年10月约5.9%的平均增速。从外部环境看，10月全球制
造业PMI为52.6%，连续15个月处于扩张区间，全球制造业仍
处上行周期为短期商品贸易提供支撑。值得注意的是，本轮制
造业扩张更多由AI、国防等领域资本开支以及投资品和中间品
需求驱动，相关需求对价格和融资成本的敏感度相对较低，因
而在高利率和通胀压力下仍保持较强韧性。与此同时，能源及
物流扰动导致全球供应商交付周期延长、积压订单增加，部分
经济体面临产能和原材料约束，我国产业链完整、生产响应较
快和交付能力稳定的优势进一步显现，有利于承接全球订单。
此外，去年同期出口基数偏低，也放大了本月同比增幅。
 亚洲区域贸易保持活力，韩国和拉美市场成为本月较为突出
的增量来源。10 月我国对东盟出口同比增长 46.3%，占出口
总额的比重升至 18.9%，对整体出口增速拉动约 8.1 个百分
点，仍是出口增长的最大拉动项。 亚洲产业链内部出现一

定分化，其中对韩国出口增速由 9 月的 68.2%进一步升至
92.4%，拉动出口增长约 3.3 个百分点，是本月主要市场中
最突出的加速项；对中国香港和印度出口分别增长 48.6%和
43.7%，继续保持较快增长。对美欧出口仍具韧性，对美国www.ccxi.com.cn

动出口增长 18.1 个百分点，较 9 月提高 0.7 个百分点。其中，集成电路出口增长

137.1%，拉动出口增长约 7.5 个百分点，仍是最主要的细分支撑；自动数据处理设
备及其零部件出口增长 114.8%，拉动作用由 9 月的 5.1 个百分点升至 6.1 个百分点，
成为本月科技产品中较为突出的增量来源。相比之下，汽车及底盘出口增速由
62.0%回落至 37.6%，拉动作用由 2.4 个百分点降至 1.8 个百分点；服装、纺织品、
玩具、家用电器、塑料制品和家具出口虽然均保持两位数增长，但合计拉动由 9 月
的约 3.8 个百分点降至2.8 个百分点。总体看，10 月出口高增更多依靠科技产业链
支撑，算力设备的拉动作用进一步增强，而汽车和传统商品贡献边际减弱，出口增
长动力较 9 月有所集中。

议升温，我国科技产品出口对全球科技投资周期的依赖进一步加深，而汽车及传统商品
增长动力有所减弱，出口增长的持续性面临一定考验。贸易政策方面，美国中期选举后
国内政策博弈可能加剧，现有关税安排仍具有阶段性，主要经济体针对重点产业的贸易
限制和供应链本地化趋势亦难以逆转。此外，中东局势反复及能源运输风险仍可能推高
生产和物流成本，进一步压缩企业利润空间。总体看，短期出口仍具较强韧性，但后续
能否保持较快增长，将更多取决于科技投资景气的持续性、传统商品需求恢复及外部贸
易环境的变化。
二、全球经济跟踪：全球经济保持温和增长，通胀黏性与政策不确定性加剧市场调整
近一个月，从宏观经济表现来看，美国经济增长加快，欧洲温和复苏，日本内需仍然偏
弱。通胀黏性与科技投资不确定性成为市场关注重点，一方面，继 9 月美联储加息后，11月
英国央行进一步加息控制通胀水平。另一方面，AI 投资回报担忧引发科技股明显调整，新兴

市场资本外流扩大。全球贸易受AI 产业链需求支撑保持韧性，但能源运输风险和地区分化仍
然突出。此外，11 月 3 日，美国中期选举民主党夺取国会两院控制权，特朗普政府施政面临
更多制约，也给外部环境带来新变局。总体看，全球经济尚具韧性，但增长动力集中、通胀
反弹及政策约束加大，经济金融运行的不确定性进一步上升。
 主要经济体增长分化，能源冲击推动生产端通胀加快上行，货币政策面临更大约
束。美国经济保持较强韧性，三季度 GDP 环比折年实际增长 3.4%，较二季度加快
1.2 个百分点，消费和投资共同支撑增长，其中 AI 相关资本开支持续扩张，但对
就业及居民收入的带动相对有限。9 月 CPI 同比上涨 3.7%，能源价格上涨加大通
胀压力。继 9 月加息 25 个基点后，美联储 10 月维持政策利率在 3.75%—4.00%，
两名委员主张进一步加息，反映通胀风险仍受到高度关注。11 月 3 日中期选举中，
民主党赢得参众两院多数席位，选民对高生活成本和中东战争的不满集中显现。
共和党失去国会控制权后，特朗普政府推动新增财政刺激、税收和其他立法议程

的难度上升，但关税及对外政策仍具有较强行政裁量空间，后续政策博弈可能加
剧经济不确定性。欧洲经济温和修复，日本内需仍显疲弱，通胀压力使得欧、日
央行货币政策维持偏紧。三季度欧元区 GDP 环比增长 0.4%，同比增长1.3%，德国
小幅回升，法国表现偏弱。10 月欧元区通胀升至 4.0%，其中能源价格同比上涨
20.8%，加剧居民购买力和企业成本压力。日本9月核心CPI同比上涨2.1%，居民

### Page 7

6
www.ccxi.com.cn
海外经济分化加深下外贸韧性能维持多久？
——2026 年 8 月进出口数据点评
消费恢复缓慢，日本央行 10 月维持政策利率 1.25%不变。总体看，中东能源冲击
使主要央行面临更突出的增长与通胀权衡，货币政策由此前的宽松预期转向维持
限制性立场甚至进一步紧缩，尤其加大了能源进口依赖型经济体的滞胀压力。
 全球金融市场波动加剧，AI 投资回报担忧引发科技股调整，能源价格与利率预期
变化推动资产重新定价。近一个月，金融市场呈现明显的结构性分化。10 月中旬
至今，标普 500 指数下跌约 3.3%，纳斯达克指数下跌约 7.7%，道琼斯指数则上涨
约 1.4%，反映资金由高估值科技股向价值和防御性板块轮动。尽管美国企业盈利
总体保持韧性，但市场对 AI 资本开支持续扩张、债务融资增加及投资回报兑现的
担忧升温，美国中期选举后数据中心建设政策的不确定性进一步加剧市场调整。
债券市场方面，避险需求上升推动美国 10 年期国债收益率由 5.2%左右降至当前
的 4.86%。大宗商品方面，中东局势反复推动油价先涨后跌，布伦特原油 10 月上

涨约13%，11月初随着地缘冲突缓和预期升温明显回落，当前降至约92美元/桶；
黄金亦呈现较大波动。总体看，近期全球资产价格调整是高利率环境下 AI 投资回
报、能源供应风险及政策不确定性共同推动的结构性重新定价。
 全球金融条件偏紧与科技股调整加剧新兴市场资本外流，但我国跨境资金仍保持
净流入。IIF 数据显示，10 月新兴市场证券投资净流出 338 亿美元，较 9 月修订
后的 193 亿美元进一步扩大，为 3 月以来最大单月流出规模，与上年同期净流入
274 亿美元形成鲜明对比。亚洲科技股调整、美元相对强势及主要经济体利率维
持高位，共同加大新兴市场资本流出压力。相比之下，我国跨境资金流动总体稳
健。国家外汇管理局数据显示，9月银行代客涉外收付款顺差407亿美元，连续六
个月保持净流入，但较8月的624亿美元下降约35%；同期银行结售汇顺差562亿
美元，反映境内外汇供求总体平衡、资金流入仍具支撑。结构上，货物贸易项下
资金保持较大规模净流入，但企业对外直接投资及境外关联企业融资有所增加。

总体看，外部金融环境收紧对我国跨境资金流动形成一定扰动，但贸易顺差及相
对稳定的市场预期仍提供支撑，需关注后续资本流入放缓及汇率波动风险。
 中东冲突仍在持续博弈拉锯中，能源运输和全球供应链仍面临较大不确定性。10
月以来，美伊冲突持续围绕霍尔木兹海峡及周边能源设施展开，11 月初多艘商船
遇袭，但卡塔尔等国也重启斡旋，局势出现一定缓和迹象。与此同时，也门政府

军在沙特支持下对胡塞武装展开反攻，争夺曼德海峡及红海沿岸关键地区控制权，
双方交战使航运安全风险持续存在。尽管部分航线逐步恢复，Lloyd's List 数据
显示，10月苏伊士运河通行船舶总载重吨升至1.26亿吨，为红海危机以来最高水
平，但仍较危机前低 33%。能源供应方面，IEA 数据显示，9 月海湾地区原油出口
已恢复至战前水平的 75%以上，但成品油和液化石油气出口仍不足战前一半，能
源供应恢复明显不均衡。展望后续，若美伊谈判取得进展、红海航运进一步恢复，
全球物流及能源成本有望回落；但若霍尔木兹海峡和曼德海峡安全形势再度恶化，
能源价格、运输成本及全球供应链稳定性仍将受到冲击，进而加大全球通胀和经
济增长压力。

Quanta Computer (2382.TW)

Quanta Computer
Company description
Founded in 1988, Quanta Computer is a Taiwan-based original design
manufacturer (ODM) of notebook (NB) PCs and other electronic hardware. It
is the world's largest NB ODM in terms of shipments, with major customers
including the leading Taiwan, US, and Japanese electronics brands. In
addition to notebooks, Quanta is expanding into cloud-related product
development, which accounted for 30-35% of total revenues in 2022.

Investment strategy
We rate Quanta at Buy. We expect stabilizing NB shipment, solid server sales
growth amid increasing AI-related applications, as well as rising EV
component exposure to drive better margins and stronger earnings growth.
The company’s attractive cash yield should also help support the shares on
the downside in case of market volatility.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$450 is based on a 14x PE multiple on 2027E earnings.
The target multiple is in line with historical trading average since 2021, mainly

to reflect strong earnings growth trajectory brought about by servers/AI
servers and EV components. We use a P/E-based methodology as we believe
the market will be primarily focused on Quanta's earnings performance given
the dynamics of the NB/server industry.
Risks
Downside risks that could prevent the stock from reaching our target price
include: 1) slower-than-expected server/AI server demand growth; 2)
execution risks relating to margin improvement such as production yield rate

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

## Document 169: 261111_daiwa_wiwynn

Source PDF: all report pdf/261111_daiwa_wiwynn.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We update our view after 3Q26 results (issued 6 November).

What’s the impact: Fundamentals largely on track. Rising CPU demand 
from agentic AI has lifted our 2026 regular server growth target to >50% 
YoY (vs. 20-30% YoY), making regular servers the key growth driver for 
Wiwynn and helping it offset the slower-than-expected AMZN ASIC AI 
server ramp in 3Q26 (ramping from September vs. original July estimate). 
Looking ahead, we expect the T3 lifecycle to likely peak in 2Q/3Q27, but 
the rising contribution from both Nvidia and AMD GPU AI servers should 
offset the slowdown in 2H27. As for the margin trend, we expect regular 
servers to be at 10% and ASIC AI servers to be at 8%. However, we expect 
the rising GPU AI server to gradually dilute the blended gross margin as 
Wiwynn focuses only on L11, while GPU motherboard (L6) and tray level 
(L10) will be handled by its parent company Wistron (3231 TT, TWD171, 
Buy [1]). As such, we expect the gross margin for Wiwynn’s GPU AI servers 
to be 2-3%, lower than the 4-5% AI of other ODM peers.

3Q26 results beat expectations. Wiwynn reported 3Q26 net income of 
TWD23.36bn (EPS: TWD41.46), beating our expectation by 8%. 3Q26 
sales (TWD434.62bn; up 56% QoQ and 63% YoY) beat our estimate by 6% 
and the Bloomberg consensus estimate by 7% on stronger-than-expected 
regular server shipments. Regular servers accounted for 50-55% of its 
revenue, down from 60-70% in 2Q26, while AI servers contributed 45-50%. 
Gross margin declined by 0.4pp QoQ to 8.9%, but still exceeded our 8.2% 
estimate. Management attributed the sequential margin contraction mainly 
to lower NRE recognition and rising material costs, as not all memory 
components used in regular servers are procured under consignment 
arrangements. However, we believe lower regular server contribution to the 
product mix was key reason. With better operating leverage, operating 
margin improved by 0.2pp QoQ to 7.5%. That said, net profit was dragged 
down by higher interest expense (reaching TWD3bn in 3Q26 vs. TWD2bn 
in 2Q26) and a higher tax rate of 23.2% (vs. 21.1% in 2Q26).

What we recommend: We lift our 2026-27E EPS by 1-5% to reflect 
stronger regular server demand but cut 2028E EPS by 3% to reflect higher 
interest expense assumption. We reaffirm our Buy (1) rating but trim our 
12M TP to TWD2,500 (from TWD2,715), based on a target PER of 13x 
(prior: 18x; within its past-2-year trading range of 9-21x) applied to our 1-
year forward diluted EPS, given weaker market sentiment. Key downside 
risks: weaker-than-expected server demand and CSP capex.

How we differ: Our 2028E EPS is 3% above street estimates due to our 
higher regular and AI server shipment assumptions.

11 November 2026

3Q26 review: fundamentals largely on track

➢ Regular server strength sustaining into 2H26, thanks to agentic AI 
➢ Increasing AI server projects from 4Q26 to boost revenue momentum 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) rating; trimming 12-month TP to TWD2,500

Company profile 
Established in 2012 and listed in 2019, Wiwynn (a subsidiary of Wistron Corp) is a dedicated ODM-
direct supplier of servers for hyperscale datacentres, with major customers including Facebook, 
Microsoft, and Amazon. Aside from hyperscaler customers, the company is expanding its target markets 
to what it terms "Next Wave" customers, including tier-2 cloud service providers and telecom operators, 
which could bring growth opportunities related to edge computing and open RAN.

### Page 5

5

Wiwynn Corp (6669 TT): 11 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 11 Nov 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
Wiwynn plans to have seven to nine directors on its board who will serve three-year terms in
accordance with the Articles of Incorporation in Taiwan. Currently, Wiwynn has 9 board
members with five independent directors. The background of independent directors is diversified
and their competence includes technology, business management, and law. We deem the
diversified background as a positive factor for the company’s board quality. Wiwynn held 9 board
meetings in 2025 and the average attendance rate was around 99%.
The Chairman does not serve concurrently as the President or CEO, which we think is a positive
for board and executive management as well as external shareholder interest.
Capital management 2
Wiwynn declared cash DPS of TWD145 and a stock dividend of TWD20 per share for 2025,
representing a total payout ratio of 60.0%. We note that investors favour its high dividend payout
ratio policy. However, Wiwynn has been expanding its own manufacturing capacity in Malaysia,
Tainan, Mexico and the US, making its business model less asset-light than before. With the
server market still in the high growth period and the payout ratio remaining at around 60%, we
expect the favourable dividend policy to be sustained going forward.
Related party &
transaction 3
In 2025, revenue from related parties accounted for 0.2% of total revenue, while purchases from
related parties were 9.2% of cost of sales. The main purchase counterparty is Wistron (3231 TT;
Wiwynn's parent company), with purchases from Wistron accounting for 8.09% of total
procurement. While Wiwynn is a professional assembly vendor for data center servers, it still
outsources a portion of its production capacity to its parent company, Wistron. At the same time,
Wiwynn has expanded its own production bases in Malaysia, Tainan, Mexico and the US,
reducing its reliance on Wistron. This business model still causes meaningful transactions with
related parties, although the exposure has declined materially. Indeed, we are concerned
whether Wistron collects fair manufacturing fees from Wiwynn. As the company states that
trading terms of purchase transactions with related parties cannot be compared with third-party
vendors due to product specifications, we are not able to assess this concern.
S Supply chain management 1
Wiwynn focuses on providing various products and system solutions for hyperscale datacentres
and cloud infrastructures. There are over 100 suppliers providing key components, electronic
materials, and mechanical materials in Wiwynn’s supply chain. For the new joiners that target to
penetrate into Wiwynn’s supply chain, Wiwynn has formed the “Supplier Evaluation Group” to
conduct: 1) supplier management and supply capabilities evaluation, 2) technical capability
development assessment, and 3) partner quality assurance (these include mainly topics
undertaken by RBA, such as no child labour, no conflict minerals, no use of environmentally
hazardous substances, etc.). New suppliers are also required to be ISO9001, ISO14001, IECQ
QC 080000, and OHSAS 18001 (ISO 45001) qualified.
Wiwynn has strong supply chain risk management by conducting audits regularly. Apart from
considering the technical skills, quality date, delivery dates and price completeness of suppliers,
Wiwynn audits its suppliers to endeavour to protect the environment, ensure safety and health of
the environment, and emphasise the importance of human rights, based on Wiwynn’s CSR
Management System.
S Data security 2
To serve world-class cloud service providers, Wiwynn has been constantly improving its
information security management. Employees are educated on the protection of the product,
technology, and information privacy of customers.
In response to the global trends in data protection laws that tend to strengthen the security of
classified information, Wiwynn has mandated that all assigned PCs have the Windows Bitlocker
HDD encryption activated to avoid classified data being accessed without permission. In 2020, it
also introduced Multi-Factor Authentication (MFA). Beyond the security measures for hardware,
the company controls access to office, labs, and conference room in a robust manner to prevent
theft of the hardware.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

All Ring Tech Co. (6187.TWO): CoWoS expansion to drive growth ahead; 
Oct rev on track; 3Q26 OP income in line; Buy
12 November 2026 | 7:57AM HKT
Oct revenues on track: Oct revenues came in at NT$978mn, +218% YoY but -16% 
MoM (vs. 3Q26 revenues +41% QoQ), accounting for 33% of our 4Q26E revenue 
forecast. Looking into 4Q26, we expect revenue to decline 11% QoQ, yet 24% ahead 
of our prior forecast on stronger-than-expected CoWoS related demand, while we 
lower our GM assumption to 50.6% (vs. 52.6% previously) to reflect a less favorable 
product mix and early stage shipments of new tools for panel level packaging and 
CPO. For 2027, we expect continued CoWoS capacity expansion at both foundry and 
OSAT customers, rising content value in panel level packaging and the ramp of the 
new CPO product line to drive growth, with GM set to recover as new products reach 
scale. Maintain Buy.

3Q26 operating income in line: Revenue was pre-announced. GM of 48.5% came in 
below GSe/Bloomberg consensus of 54.9%/54.3%, which we attribute to product 
mix, with a higher contribution from lower-margin automation equipment. However, 
with a larger revenue scale and a lower opex ratio of 15.6% (vs. GSe/cons. of 
18.2%/17.7%), operating income grew 39% QoQ, 2% / 1% behind GSe/cons., largely 
in line. Below the operating line, All Ring booked a non-operating loss of NT$55mn in 
3Q26 (vs. non-operating income of NT$174mn in 2Q26), primarily attributable to 
valuation losses from equity investment, partly reversing the gains booked in 2Q26, 
which led EPS (NT$8.49) to miss by 10% vs GSe.

### Page 2

CoWoS expansion and new product ramps to drive growth: We expect 4Q26E 
revenue to decline 11% QoQ, still well ahead of our prior forecast on CoWoS-related 
demand. Our forecast implies average monthly revenue of NT$983mn in 
November-December (broadly flat vs. October). On the margin front, we expect GM to 
remain subject to product mix, with new tools carrying lower margins in the initial ramp. 
Longer term, we remain positive on All Ring, and expect 2027-28E growth to be driven 
by 1) continued CoWoS capacity expansion at both foundry and OSAT customers; 2) 
panel-level packaging, which brings higher-ASP tools and additional process steps, 
lifting content value per line; 3) the ramp of its new CPO product line, spanning fiber 
coupling and optical engine inspection tools, which we expect to start contributing 
meaningfully in 2027; and 4) new customer penetration, including a US customer’s 
advanced packaging capacity.

Earnings revision: We incorporate the 3Q26 results into our model and revise our 
2026/2027/2028E EPS by +2.7%/0.0%/+1.7%, as 1) higher revenue 
(+9.4%/+1.4%/+3.6% vs. our prior forecasts) on stronger CoWoS-related demand and 2) 
a lower opex ratio on the larger revenue base are offset by 3) lower GM, reflecting the 
product mix and the initial ramp of new products, and 4) the non-operating loss in 
3Q26. Net net, we expect All Ring’s revenue to grow 87%/33%/29% YoY in 2026-28E 
(vs. 71%/44%/26% previously), with GM of 51.0%/53.1%/53.5% (vs. 
53.8%/54.2%/54.9% previously).

Valuation: We maintain our 12-month TP at NT$1,450 (implied 35x 2027E PE vs. 36% 
NI YoY of 2027-28E on avg.), still based on a target P/E multiple of 35x (unchanged; 
derived by applying the implied earnings growth uplift (per GSe) to the company’s 
historical valuation framework) applied to our FY27E (unchanged) EPS. We view CPO as 
an even stronger structural tailwind for All Ring than CoWoS, with the potential to drive 
multiple-fold earnings expansion over the next several years, further supporting both 
earnings visibility and scope for valuation re-rating. Maintain Buy.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

### Page 1

M
Update
Compal Electronics  | Asia Pacific
Server Ramp Takes Shape but
Valuation Keeps Us UW
Compal’s server expansion is progressing, with revenue
contribution rising to ~12% in 3Q and management targeting 30-
40% in CY27. However, weaker PC volumes and diminishing
scale benefits continue to pressure margins. At ~14x CY27E P/E,
valuation remains stretched.
3Q OpM missed. See our  3Q26 Results Summary                   .
PC business facing demand and profitability headwinds: Compal expects a mid-

teens q/q decline in 4Q26 shipments because of higher selling prices. This weakness
should persist into CY27; the company projects a likely double-digit y/y unit decline,
though it admitted that visibility remains low. However, rising ASPs from mix
changes and cost pass-through could keep 4Q PC revenue up sequentially.
Servers have become a key growth driver: Servers rose from ~5% of the mix in 1Q
to ~12% in 3Q, with a CY27 revenue contribution target of 30-40%. While 4Q26

server revenue is guided to be flat q/q, Compal is aggressively scaling its capacity. It
now primarily ships from Taiwan, and a major L10/11 ramp will begin in 2Q27 at its
new Texas plant. It currently does L10 HGX for Neocloud customers and L6 for a US
enterprise customer; it will do L10/11 for enterprise customer(s) as well, and is
actively approaching CSP customers for ASIC L11 solutions as well.
Margins to remain pressured: Overall margins were pressured by the PC segment,

driving a sequential decline in 3Q owing to lower PC volumes and utilization. This
margin headwind should intensify in 4Q because of shrinking PC business scale.
Conversely, server gross margin should remain resilient in the high single digits to
10%,  staying flat q/q.
4Q preview: We estimate that 4Q revenue will grow ~8% q/q, to NT$263bn (+38%
y/y), primarily driven by higher PC ASPs despite declining volumes. GM should
contracting ~10bps q/q, to 4.3% (-150bps y/y), with declining PC profitability, mainly

owing to diminishing scale benefits. This leads to our OP estimate of NT$2.8bn
(+25% q/q, +6% y/y) and NP estimate of NT$1.7bn (-34% q/q, +21% y/y), for EPS of
NT$0.39. Our 4Q net profit estimate is down q/q because of lower non-op income.
Stay UW with price target unchanged at NT$23.0 (10.4x CY27 P/E). We think
valuation looks quite stretched now at ~14x CY27e P/E. We prefer other ODMs like
Wistron (6x), Hon Hai (12x), Wiwynn (8x), or Quanta (9x).

Morgan Stanley appreciates your support in
the 2027 Extel Asia Research Survey.
Request your ballot                          here.

3Q26 Results Summary
3Q26 gross margin miss:
• Revenue was NT$243bn (+2% q/q, +30% y/y), and GM was 4.4% (-30bps q/q, -
130bps y/y), 80bps and 40bps below our estimate and the Street, respectively,
driven by lower PC margins.
• As a result, OP was NT$2.bn (-21% q/q, -16% y/y), 33% below our estimate/34%
be;low the Street, for OpM of 0.9% (-30bps q/q, -50bps y/y).
• Non-op profit was higher than expected, so net profit reached Nt$2.6bn (-18% q/q,

+33% y/y), 15% above our estimate/2% above the Street, for EPS of NT$0.59.

### Page 4

M
Update
4
Price Target and Valuation Methodology
Our 12-month price target is unchanged at  NT$23.0: Our price target is our base case
scenario value, derived from a multistage residual income (RI) model. We use the
company's beginning equity plus the present value of all expected residual income in
excess of the cost of capital. The RI is positive when ROAE is above the cost of capital and
negative when it is below.
We assume a cost of equity of 9.1%  (beta of 1.3, equity risk premium of 6.0% and risk-free

rate of 1.5%), a medium-term growth rate of 5%, and a terminal growth rate of 3% (all
unchanged).
Our new price target implies a 2026e P/E of 10.4x and a 2027e P/E of 8.5x, below the five-
year average of 13.8x. We view these as fair, because we factor in more conservative
assumptions for its consumer business and a weaker margin outlook for Compal. The
company's venture into the "new businesses" (e.g., auto, server, etc.) is constraining its
margin profile as it tries to shift from 80%+ PC to more commercial exposure.

Our bull and bear case values are also unchanged, at NT$38.0 and NT$17.0,
respectively.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Hon Hai Precision (2317.TW)
Base case scenario value, derived from a residual income valuation model. Our key assump-
tions include a cost of equity of 8.5%, a medium-term growth rate of 13%, and a terminal
growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected iPhone sell-through
n Faster progress in AI server business
n Faster EV business development progress
n Any new M&A activity that could improve sentiment

Risks to Downside
n Lower iPhone sell-through
n Smaller profit contribution from AI business
n Slower EV business development progress
n Geopolitical developments that could negatively affect foreign investment
Quanta Computer Inc. (2382.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.0% (beta of
1.2, equity premium of 6.0% and risk-free rate of 1.5%), a 7.0% medium-term growth rate, and
a 3% terminal growth rate.

Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected NB demand
n Stronger-than-expected Apple Watch demand
n Stronger-than-expected server demand
n Faster-than-expected AI server penetration
Risks to Downside
n Weaker-than-expected NB demand
n Softer-than-expected Apple Watch demand
n Weak margin performance owing to rising labor costs and sales shortfalls
n Fierce price competition in the mega data center segment
n Slower-than-expected AI server penetration

Wistron Corporation (3231.TW)
Base case, residual income valuation. Key assumptions: 8.7% cost of equity, 5.0% medium-
term growth rate and 3% terminal growth rate.
Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected divestiture of consumer electronics business
n Stronger-than-expected NB demand
n Margin expansion from better product mix

Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

M
Update
Co-Tech Development Corp.  | Asia Pacific
Key Japanese Competitor
Announced Price Hikes
What's new? Our supply chain checks suggest that one of Co-Tech's key Japanese
competitors notified customers earlier this week of price increases for its
electrodeposited (ED) copper foil products. The company will raise prices by 30%
for ¼ oz (9µm) and ⅓ oz (12µm) ED copper foils and by 15% for other thicknesses,
effective for shipments from December 2026 onward. This aligns with our previous

supply chain check findings published in our Nov 4 report (link).
Our view: The increases extend beyond high-end HVLP products to copper foils
used in consumer electronics. We believe this reflects rising production costs and,
more importantly, tightening supply, with positive implications for Co-Tech. We
expect Co-Tech to follow with further price increases, although likely at smaller
magnitudes given the ~10% hike it implemented in October.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+

Rachel L Zhang
Equity Analyst

Key Takeaways
Earlier this week, a key Japanese competitor issued a price increase notice to its
customers for electrodeposited copper foils.
This aligns with our  supply chain check findings published in our Nov 4 report.
We believe the  price hikes have positive implications for Co-Tech and expect Co-
Tech to follow with price increases as well.

However, since Co-Tech already implemented a ~10% price hike in October, we
don't expect it to announce a price hike of the same magnitude.

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 262: [012450] 한화에어로스페이스(012450) 여전히 비중확대 의견, 그리고 Top-Pick한화에어로스페이스(012450) 여전히 비중확대 의견, 그리고 Top-Pick_cd366e43

Source PDF: all report pdf/[012450] 한화에어로스페이스(012450) 여전히 비중확대 의견, 그리고 Top-Pick한화에어로스페이스(012450) 여전히 비중확대 의견, 그리고 Top-Pick_cd366e43.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

(012450) 
한화에어로스페이스 
2026.10.30

AI 로보틱스/방산/조선/운송 양승윤 
6139 / syyang0901@eugenefn.com

여전히 비중확대 의견, 그리고 Top-Pick 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 1,660,000 원(유지) 
 현재주가 760,000 원(10/29)

 3Q26 연결 매출액은 6조 943억원(-6.0%yoy), 영업이익은 4,520억원(-
46.2%yoy, OPM 7.4%)을 기록. 영업이익 컨센 1조 1,614억원을 하회.  
 지상방산의 손익이 크게 악화. 영업이익은 217억원(OPM 1.7%) 기록. 주요 
납품이 4분기에 집중된 가운데 일회성 비용 약 500억원(대전사업장 복구·
정상화 약 300억원, 국내 사업 충당금 약 160억원)과 환율 영향이 겹침.   
 다만 내용을 살펴보면 수출 사업의 수익성 훼손은 크지 않았다는 판단임.  
 일회성 제외 영업이익은 약 717억원(OPM 5.8%)이며, 일회성 비용은 대전
사업장이 국내 물량을 전담한다는 점에서 전액 국내 귀속으로 추정. 내수 
OPM을 일회성 제외 -5% 내외로 가정하면, 내수 영업이익은 약 -826억원
(일회성 포함 OPM -12.7%), 수출은 1,043억원(OPM 17.6%)으로 추정됨. 
 1Q26 대비로는 국내 마진 하락이, 2Q26 대비로는 수출 물량 감소가 가장 
크게 작용(도표 참고). 국내는 저마진 사업 매출이 늘고 고마진 반복 양산 물
량이 줄며 일시적으로 믹스가 악화. 수출은 폴란드 등 주요 인도가 정체되어 
매출이 감소한 가운데 저수익 사업 중심으로 믹스가 악화된 영향. 
 4Q26부터 회복 구간 진입 전망. 폴란드 K9 2차 실행계약 물량의 현지 인
도가 본격화되며, 회사는 2026년 폴란드 K9 30문 이상, 천무 발사대 60대 
이상(*연초 40대에서 상향) 인도를 계획. 수출입 데이터상으로도 폴란드·루
마니아향 출하가 대규모로 진행 중이며 폴란드향 부속류 수출도 정상적으로 
이어지는 것으로 확인. 일회성 소멸과 고마진 수출 집중으로 수출 OPM은 
30% 내외로 회복할 것. 다만 국내는 정상 가동(2027년 상반기 목표) 전까
지 한 자릿수 초반 마진에 머물 것으로 전망. 
 중장기 수익성 우려도 크지 않다는 판단. 2027~2029년 매출 연 15% 성
장 가이던스는 유지됐고, 내년 폴란드 K9·천무 인도 물량은 모두 올해 대비 
증가할 계획. 폴란드 사업 이후 높아진 수출 마진 레벨은 후속 사업에서도 
유지될 전망. 수주는 중동 지정학 불안으로 지연되고 있으나 대공체계 등 문
의가 이어지고 있어, 2027년부터 중동이 수주의 중심이 될 것으로 기대. 
 동사에 대한 투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 166만원을 유지함. 3Q26 지상방산 
쇼크는 국내 적자와 수출 물량 공백이 겹친 일시적 결과로, 수출 경쟁력 훼
손으로 보기 어려움. 실적 충격에 따른 주가 조정은 분할 매수 기회로 판단.  
 한화에어로스페이스의 주가는 4월 7일 사상 최고가 153.7만원에서 10월 
29일 76.0만원으로 -51% 조정 받은 상황. 12MF P/E 기준 14.3x, 27E 
P/E는 13.9x로 밸류에이션 관점에서도 매력적인 구간 진입. 그동안 3분기 
실적 우려 등으로 부진했다면, 이제는 실적 바텀아웃과 대형 수주 랠리 속 
다시 한 번 반등을 기록할 차례. 동사를 방산업종 Top-pick으로 유지함.

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

투자기간 및 투자등급/투자의견 비율 
종목추천 및 업종추천 투자기간: 12 개월  (추천기준일 종가대비 추천종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함) 당사 투자의견 비율(%) 
ㆍSTRONG BUY(매수) 추천기준일 종가대비 +50%이상 3% 
ㆍBUY(매수) 추천기준일 종가대비 +15%이상 ~ +50%미만 94% 
ㆍHOLD(중립) 추천기준일 종가대비 -10%이상 ∼ +15%미만 3% 
ㆍREDUCE(매도) 추천기준일 종가대비 -10%미만 0% 
(2026.09.30 기준)

## Document 282: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

(329180) 
HD 현대중공업 
2026.10.29

AI 로보틱스/방산/조선/운송 양승윤 
6139 / syyang0901@eugenefn.com

피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 610,000 원(하향) 
 현재주가 285,500 원(10/28)

 3Q26 매출액은 5조 7,536억원(+30.2%yoy), 영업이익은 8,992억원
(+61.3%yoy, OPM 15.6%)을 기록. 영업이익 컨센서스 9,792억원 하회.  
 평균 환율이 약 79원 하락하며 영업이익 약 450억원이 줄었고, 조업
일수는 47일로 통상 계절성 대비 4일 추가 감소해 약 500억원의 이
익 감소 효과 발생. 임단협 추가 비용의 약 75%도 3분기에 선반영
(잔여분은 4분기 반영). 일회성 요인을 제외하면 이익 체력은 견조. 
 동사의 조선 부문 매출액은 4조 3,988억원(+44.0%yoy), 영업이익은 
6,463억원(+62.8%yoy, OPM 14.7%)을 기록. 상선 OPM은 14.7%로 
2Q26(17.8%) 대비 하락했으나 대부분 외생변수 영향. 3Q26 대형선 매출 
중 2023년 수주분 비중은 45%, 2024~2025년 수주분은 51%. 고선가 
물량의 매출 비중이 높아지는 2027년까지 수익성 개선 여력은 충분.  
 9월 말 누적 상선 수주는 138.0억달러로 연간 목표의 120%, 엔진기계는 
41.4억달러로 155%를 달성했으나 함정(13%)과 해양(5%)은 부진. LNG선
은 중국 조선소의 저가 수주와 발주 둔화로 선가가 일부 하락. 선주들이 현 
선가를 저점으로 인식하며 발주 협의를 재개하는 분위기. 북미 LNG 프로젝
트 FID가 이어지고 있어 2027년 LNG선 발주 회복 기대. 
 함정 부문은 매출 2,570억원(-14.6%qoq)으로 줄었으나, 수출 비중 확대에 
따른 믹스 개선으로 영업이익 386억원(+114%qoq, OPM 15.0%)을 기록. 
해양플랜트는 매출액 3,799억원(+35.3%yoy), 영업이익 1,265억원(OPM 
33.3%). C/O 180억원을 제외해도 Ruya 공정 본격화와 Trion 고수익 물량
으로 높은 수익성 유지. 2027년 일부 프로젝트 인도로 매출 감소가 예상되
나, LNG선·블록 물량 투입으로 고정비 부담을 완화할 계획. 신규 해상변전
소(OSS) 계약을 앞두고 있으며, 2027년 초 추가 수주 발표도 기대. 
 주목할 부분은 엔진기계. 영업이익 2,299억원(OPM 24.2%)으로 높은 D/F 
엔진 비중을 바탕으로 견조한 수익성 유지. 육상발전용 힘센엔진은 올해   
3GW를 확보하며 2029년까지 계획 캐파 수준의 물량을 이미 채움. 생산 
예상 물량은 2028년 약 1.5GW, 2029년 약 3GW이며, 신규 공장 구축 이
후 연간 생산능력은 약 4GW로 확대 예정. 북미 Hyperscaler와의 직접 협
상이 늘고 있으며, 힘센엔진을 활용한 부유식 발전설비(BMPP)로 FDC 시장
까지 대응 가능. AI 데이터센터 전력 수요가 2028년 이후 새로운 이익 축. 
 미국에서는 Kiewit과 합작법인 설립 계약 체결. 군수지원함 등 지원함 사업
부터 추진하며, 현지 조선소 건설 과정에서 HII에 선박 블록을 공급하는 방
안도 협의 중. 구체적인 투자계획은 2026년 말까지 확정 목표. 국내에서는 
핵추진잠수함 초기 예산이 2027년 반영되며 동사도 사업 참여를 준비 중. 
 동사에 대한 투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 61만원을 제시. 고선가 물량 매출 확
대와 육상발전용 엔진 성장으로 피크아웃을 이겨낼 굴지의 조선사.

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX tăng điểm trong tuần qua, +68.24 điểm tương ứng +4.00%, với thanh khoản cải 
thiện. Thiết bị điện, Khách sạn - Nhà hàng và Thiết bị dịch vụ y tế là các nhóm ngành có 
mức tăng rõ rệt nhất trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần 
trước với giá trị -966 tỷ đồng, tập trung ở các mã FPT, ACB và VCB; mặt khác, tự doanh duy 
trì mua ròng ở mức 137 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều mua ròng 1,335 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua nhưng đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -3,049 hợp đồng. Tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng 
1,815 hợp đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -6,000 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam hồi phục trở lại sau bốn tuần giảm liên tiếp, khi VN-Index bật tăng mạnh 
từ vùng hỗ trợ quanh 1.700 điểm với thanh khoản cải thiện. Sau nhịp điều chỉnh kéo dài 
khiến mặt bằng định giá về vùng hấp dẫn, lực cầu bắt đáy đã nhập cuộc quyết liệt hơn, 
với phiên 21/10 tăng gần 47 điểm — một trong những phiên tăng mạnh nhất nhiều tháng 
gần đây. Điểm tích cực và đáng chú ý nhất là khối ngoại đã xuất hiện hai phiên đảo chiều 
mua ròng trở lại sau khoảng 20 phiên bán ròng liên tiếp, tập trung vào nhóm Bluechip 
như VIC, TCB, VHM, MSN — tín hiệu đầu tiên cho thấy áp lực rút vốn ngoại có thể đang dần 
hạ nhiệt. Thông tin tích cực tác động lên thị trường đến từ các chỉ đạo của NHNN và Chính 
phủ về việc hạ mặt bằng lãi suất để hỗ trợ tăng trưởng kinh tế. Bên cạnh đó, mùa báo cáo 
KQKD quý 3 khả quan đang diễn ra cũng là yếu tố hỗ trợ xu hướng thị trường chung.

19/10/2026

Nguồn: V_KBSource

Triển vọng kinh tế 4Q2026 
Giữ nhịp tăng trưởng, vững vàng về itch

Một số dự báo chính của KBSV về kinh tế Việt Nam trong năm 2026:

1) Tăng trưởng GDP năm 2026 được dự báo sẽ đạt 9.0%, với động lực chính 
đến từ định hướng đẩy mạnh giải ngân đầu tư công của Chính phủ, dòng 
vốn FDI duy trì tích cực và hoạt động sản xuất công nghiệp phục hồi theo 
xuất khẩu. Về rủi ro, chúng tôi cho rằng Việt Nam sẽ tiếp tục đối mặt với 
một số yếu tố bất định từ bên ngoài, bao gồm: rủi ro xung đột Mỹ – Iran 
kéo dài hơn dự kiến, xu hướng tăng của mặt bằng lãi suất toàn cầu gây gia 
tăng áp lực tài chính và kéo giảm tổng cầu, cùng nguy cơ vỡ bong bóng AI.

2) Chúng tôi nâng dự báo lạm phát bình quân năm 2026 của Việt Nam lên 
mức 4.7% YoY, cao hơn mục tiêu kiểm soát lạm phát của Chính phủ, dựa 
trên kịch bản dự báo thận trọng hơn về diễn biến xung đột địa chính trị và 
giá năng lượng thế giới trong ngắn hạn.

3) Mặt bằng lãi suất trong quý IV được dự báo sẽ ổn định quanh vùng hiện 
tại và không xuất hiện thêm nhịp tăng đột biến, nhờ dòng tiền cải thiện 
từ giải ngân đầu tư công và kỳ vọng có thêm các biện pháp hỗ trợ thanh 
khoản trung dài hạn từ phía Chính phủ và NHNN.

4) Tỷ giá USD/VND trong quý cuối năm có thể chịu một số sức ép do Fed dự 
báo còn thêm một lần tăng lãi suất nữa trong năm. Dù vậy, chúng tôi cho 
rằng tỷ giá USD/VND khó tăng mạnh mà chỉ quay lại mức đầu năm, dựa 
trên kỳ vọng chỉ số DXY không biến động mạnh, nguồn cung ngoại tệ cải 
thiện (FDI giải ngân, kiều hối, nâng hạng TTCK) và chênh lệch lãi suất 
VND–USD duy trì trạng thái dương.

Tham khảo nội dung chi tiết trong báo cáo tại đây.

### Page 4

4

21/10/2026

CTCP VÀNG BẠC ĐÁ QUÝ PHÚ NHUẬN (PNJ)  
Cập nhật ĐHĐCĐ bất thường 21/10/2026

Tái cơ cấu để xử lý áp lực thanh khoản  
— ĐHĐCĐ bất thường PNJ ngày 21/10/2026 đã thông qua điều chỉnh kế hoạch kinh 
doanh và phương án phát hành riêng lẻ để bổ sung vốn tái cấu trúc. Ban lãnh đạo đặt 
ra các vấn đề trọng yếu là đáp ứng nhu cầu thanh khoản và chuẩn bị nguồn lực để thực 
hiện các cam kết thu đổi đã tích lũy trong quá khứ. Bên cạnh đó, các cổ đông tham dự 
cũng đặt các câu hỏi liên quan đến tình hình hoạt động qua các điểm chính (1) dòng 
tiền hoạt động kinh doanh; (2) diễn biến thị trường sau khi PNJ áp dụng chính sách thu 
đổi mới; (3) hiệu quả hoạt động sau tái cấu trúc.

Nội dung chính tại đại hội 
— Theo Ban lãnh đạo, thông tin liên quan đến P-Lab từ đầu tháng 7 khiến khách hàng gia 
tăng bán lại sản phẩm. Tổng giá trị thu đổi và mua lại trong tháng đạt gần 6,000 tỷ VND, 
so với khoảng 7,000 tỷ VND trong cả năm 2025. Theo BCTC riêng, nguồn tiền khả dụng 
giảm từ khoảng 3,700 tỷ VND cuối Q2/2026 xuống 770 tỷ VND cuối Q3/2026, đã bao 
gồm khoản vay 700 tỷ VND từ Chủ tịch HĐQT. Tỷ trọng kim cương và trang sức kim 
cương trong tổng tồn kho tăng từ 41% lên 52%. 
— Q3/2026 ghi nhận lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND trước phần tăng trích lập dự phòng. Để giảm 
áp lực thanh toán, PNJ kéo dài thời gian chi trả đối với kim cương lên 120 ngày từ tháng 
07/2026. Thay đổi chính sách cũng ảnh hưởng đến niềm tin khách hàng và sức mua, cụ 
thể doanh thu và lợi nhuận gộp Q3/2026 đạt lần lượt 6,995/609 tỷ VND, giảm 14%/62% 
YoY. Chi phí bán hàng và quản lý giảm 13% YoY chưa đủ bù đắp mức giảm lợi nhuận 
gộp, khiến PNJ lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND, so với lãi 496 tỷ VND trong Q3/2025. 
— PNJ nâng giả định tỷ lệ thu đổi trên giá trị thu đổi còn lại lên 80%, so với 28.8% đối với 
nhóm sản phẩm giai đoạn 2020–2026 trong ước tính tại BCTC bán niên 2026. Tổng dự 
phòng tổn thất dự kiến tăng từ 2,267 tỷ VND lên 7,071 tỷ VND. Kế hoạch điều chỉnh ghi 
nhận doanh thu 39,057 tỷ VND, lỗ trước thuế 6,059 tỷ VND và lỗ sau thuế 6,271 tỷ VND. 
Theo Ban lãnh đạo, LNST loại trừ dự phòng ước khoảng 800 tỷ VND, chủ yếu nhờ kết 
quả Q1/2026. Khoản dự phòng phản ánh tổn thất ước tính cho toàn bộ kịch bản, không 
đồng nghĩa toàn bộ giá trị sẽ được chi trả ngay trong kỳ. 
— Giá trị cam kết thu đổi tích lũy đối với kim cương và trang sức kim cương tại cuối 
Q3/2026 được PNJ ước tính khoảng 21,000 tỷ VND. Với hàng đã bán, doanh nghiệp dự 
kiến chuyển đổi quyền lợi khách hàng sang sản phẩm và các chương trình ưu đãi. Với 
sản phẩm bán mới, chính sách thu đổi định hướng theo giá thị trường tại thời điểm 
giao dịch, thay cho tỷ lệ cố định trên giá trị hóa đơn, nhằm hạn chế tiếp tục tích lũy 
nghĩa vụ tiềm tàng trong tương lai theo cơ chế cũ. 
Tham khảo nội dung chi tiết trong báo cáo tại đây.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX hình thành cây nến xanh thân dài trên khung tuần và đáng chú ý là cho phản ứng 
bật tăng ngay tại cạnh dưới của mẫu hình tam giác đi ngang trung hạn, cho thấy tín hiệu 
tích cực đã quay trở lại với xu hướng ngắn hạn. Trên khung ngày, sau 2 phiên điều chỉnh, 
rung lắc vào đầu tuần, chỉ số cũng đảo chiều tăng điểm khá dốc, với 3 cây nến xanh liên 
tiếp với thanh khoản được tăng cường, củng cố thêm tín hiệu xác nhận cho đà hồi phục. 
Sau nhịp bật tăng mạnh từ đáy, VNINDEX được dự báo sẽ sớm gặp phải áp lực điều chỉnh 
khi quay lên điểm giữa của dải đi ngang theo mẫu hình tam giác, tương ứng vùng kháng cự 
kế tiếp tại quanh 1810. Tuy nhiên, xu hướng ngắn hạn vẫn có phần nghiêng về chiều tăng, 
với tín hiệu xác nhận đáy của một số mã cổ phiếu sau quá trình sụt giảm kéo dài.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên)

## Document 368: FX Daily_ Don’t mess with the dollar near term

Source PDF: all report pdf/FX Daily_ Don’t mess with the dollar near term.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-13
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Hot US inﬂation and the prospect of a more aggressive
response from the Fed are the themes dominating FX markets
currently. Another dose of ﬁrm US PPI today and some more
hawkish Fed rhetoric are set to keep the dollar very supported
into next week

Chris Turner, Frantisek Taborsky and Francesco Pesole
October US PPI data today should keep short-dated yields and the dollar bid
Please see ING's FX Outlook 2027: Fat tails, slim ranges for
insights into key themes next year.
USD: Do not stand in the way of the
dollar
This week has seen a powerful, macro-driven rally in the dollar.
Short-dated US yields are close to 40bp higher since Tuesday
morning, when the Federal Reserve's Christopher Waller mused
over the need for a 50bp hike in December. The market has since

### Page 2

adopted the view that back-to-back December and January hikes
look likely, while extending the full Fed adjustment from here to a
full 85bp of tightening. That's beginning to look a lot more like a
cycle than the kind of modest adjustment we recently presented in
our house baseline view. Notably, a few Fed members are starting
to describe conditions more as a demand than a supply shock.
It is hard to see what is going to change this hawkish Fed/dollar-

positive mindset in the next few weeks. The Fed centrists might try
to emphasise the need for a gradual adjustment in policy, but it is
probably only a big US tech sell-oﬀ that can put a dent in short-
dated US yields now.
In terms of the FX reaction, the low-yielding Swiss franc and the yen
have some of the highest correlations with US rates and are under
pressure. 0.8400 is a really important resistance area for USD/CHF
and will probably help determine whether the dollar needs to take

another big leg higher over the coming weeks. USD/JPY is already
trading through the psychological level at 160, and we would not
expect any intervention yet given the macro nature of the broadly
stronger dollar. As we said on Wednesday, we expect to see dollar
strength more expressed against activity currencies, where
something like the low-yielding Swedish krona looks vulnerable.
For today, the October US PPI looks likely to keep the narrative of

hot US inﬂation alive. Expect scrutiny into the components of PPI,
such as healthcare, airfares and software, that feed into the Fed's
preferred PCE inﬂation data – the next release of which is on 25
November. Were that core PCE to rise 0.3% month-on-month for the
third month in a row, expectations of a 50bp December hike would
grow.
DXY has broken to a new high for the year and looks like it can push
on to the next resistance level at 104.60. That's about a 1.5%

advance from current levels and would be consistent with EUR/USD
trading around 1.0880.
Chris Turner
EUR: ECB struggles to outhawk the Fed

European Central Bank rhetoric ﬁrmly points to the need for a rate
hike in December. The problem for EUR/USD is that the market sees
the potential for a prolonged tightening cycle as more credible in
the US than in the eurozone. You don't hear many ECB speakers
talking of a demand shock in Europe. Short-dated EUR/USD swap
diﬀerentials have moved out to the widest levels of the year and are
clearly weighing on the pair.
Given the run of expected US inﬂation data over the coming weeks,

we struggle to see what can take the pressure oﬀ EUR/USD. A break
below 1.10 is not a given, but that level is certainly at risk into year-
end. EUR/USD bulls will have to be hoping for either an AI crash, the
ECB sounding far more hawkish or perhaps some major
geopolitical breakthrough in Iran or Ukraine.
In focus today will be the second release of third-quarter eurozone
GDP data. This printed a respectable 0.4% quarter-on-quarter
previously. And there are also several ECB speakers – all likely to

point to a December rate hike.
Chris Turner
CEE: The pressure on FX is far from over
Friday will bring more data to the CEE region than the relatively
quiet days we have seen this week. This morning, Q3 GDP ﬁgures
for Romania and Poland will be released. While the former is
expected to show some acceleration, the latter is likely to reﬂect a
slowdown. Poland will also release its ﬁnal inﬂation reading for
October, expected at 3.5%, and on Monday, we'll receive the core

inﬂation ﬁgure, which we estimate has slowed to 2.8%.
In Turkey, inﬂation expectations for November will be published.
Yesterday, the Central Bank of Turkey revised its inﬂation forecast
for next year from 15% to 21%, bringing it closer to the current
market expectation of 23% but also opening the door to a
December rate cut. The Czech National Bank will release the
minutes from last week’s meeting, during which it raised interest
rates by 25bp to 4.00%. However, the 4-3 vote split sent a dovish

The CEE market remains under pressure. Yesterday’s surge in

BRL: Lula trying to take some ﬁscal
action
The Brazilian real has slumped since incumbent President Luiz

Inácio Lula da Silva beat Flavio Bolsonaro in the run-oﬀ vote last
month. At the heart of the concern is the ﬁscal side, where Lula's
pre-election proposals have only added to concern about Brazil's
ﬁscal position. Over the last few days, however, Lula's government
has started to propose some ﬁscal consolidation for the 2027
budget, and we presume more is coming.
While Brazil's ﬁscal story is vulnerable, its monetary story is
supportive for the real. High real interest rates and an independent

central bank make the Brazilian real an expensive sell. We look for a
recovery in 2027. Please see the Latam section in our 2027 FX
Outlook for more.
Chris Turner

## Document 379: How the blue wave could reshape US energy and data centres

Source PDF: all report pdf/How the blue wave could reshape US energy and data centres.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-05
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Energy aﬀordability shaped the midterms. A divided
government will now face pressure to shield consumers from
rising data centre power costs while speeding up new energy
supply. Onshoring and trade protections will continue, despite
higher near-term costs, as energy security remains a priority
Coco Zhang
After the midterm elections, the government will need to minimise data
centre-related costs for consumers, bring new power supply online faster and

protect supply chains. Pictured: Data centre in Ohio
Exit polls from the US midterm elections highlighted a key concern
shaping voters’ decisions: energy aﬀordability. Nearly 90% of
voters said the cost of living was a crucial or major factor in their
vote for Congress, with many pointing to gasoline and utility costs.
Meanwhile, as the AI data centre boom strains power supply and
infrastructure, 56% said the issue was a crucial or major factor in

their vote.
With Democrats now set to control both the House and Senate,
what does this mean for US energy and data centre policy?

### Page 2

Newly elected lawmakers will face pressure to address these
concerns, requiring the Democratic Congress and Republican White
House to ﬁnd common ground on shielding consumers from data
centre-related costs and accelerating new power supply, including
through permitting reform. Onshoring and supply chain protections
are also likely to continue. While they can raise near-term costs,
they remain important to US energy security. Developments in all
three areas could have signiﬁcant implications for businesses.

That said, Congress needs to strike a balance when drafting
legislation aimed at achieving these goals. More aggressive
measures could face a veto from President Trump.
Strengthened data centre regulation ahead,
but the devil is in the detail
The data centre industry is facing heightened local opposition.
Recent polls suggest that around one in seven Americans is
concerned about or opposes data centre development in their
neighbourhoods.
In response to this issue, there is likely to be tighter data centre

regulation. State-level policy will be more important to watch, as
action is politically and practically easier at this level. The most
likely approach is targeted regulation, much of which is already
emerging. This includes data centre-related taxes, reduced tax
incentives, environmental standards, and requirements for
developers to bear relevant cost increases. Outright moratoriums
remain unlikely to become mainstream. Where they are introduced,

they are more likely to be temporary, giving states time to develop
a data centre policy framework.
At the federal level, the key unanswered question is how much
authority should remain with the states and whether federal
legislation should mandate state regulations or simply provide
guidance. Republicans generally favour letting states lead, while
Democrats tend to support a stronger federal role.
We think sweeping data centre legislation is unlikely because of

President Trump’s veto power. Federal action is more likely to focus

on aﬀordability by protecting electricity customers from higher
costs and formalising data centres' responsibility to help fund grid
and energy infrastructure upgrades. Such measures could be added
to broader legislation, such as must-pass spending bills needed to
avoid a government shutdown, or a bipartisan package like
permitting reform.
More broadly on AI, we expect the Democratic Party to increase
scrutiny of technology companies, as discussed here, while utilising

the next two years to prepare an AI governance framework for 2028
and beyond.
However, tighter AI and data centre regulation is unlikely to
fundamentally alter the industry’s growth trajectory. AI’s
importance to US economic growth and competition with China will
remain powerful drivers of expansion.
Business implications
State-level policy would more directly aﬀect project siting,
which is important for data centre companies to watch.
Where state rules are tighter, project development could slow.

However, a more restrictive regulatory environment could
also increase the value of data centres already approved or
under construction, easing some of the industry’s capital
constraints. Higher valuations could also encourage
developers to be more selective and expand at a more
measured pace, ultimately improving the quality of the
project pipeline.
Developers should also treat community engagement as a
strategic investment. Recent surveys show that while only

20-30% of Americans support local data centre construction,
support can rise to 50-70% when there are accompanying
community investments, such as in public infrastructure
improvements or reductions in residents' costs. More
developers are now signing binding community beneﬁt
agreements as part of project development.

Enabling faster power generation through
permitting reform
Both parties support timely expansion of electricity supply to meet

rising demand from AI and manufacturing reshoring. They also
generally agree that energy abundance can help reduce cost
pressures. While Democrats and Republicans diﬀer on the preferred
energy mix, there is still common ground. Technologies likely to
retain bipartisan support include nuclear, geothermal, carbon
capture, and biofuels.
However, the greatest potential for policy action may lie not in
speciﬁc technologies, but in permitting reform.
Today, many energy projects can spend over ﬁve years navigating

the federal permitting process. Around $240bn to $280bn of
infrastructure investment across the eight sectors below enters the

With both parties recognising the need for reform, the Senate
introduced the Bipartisan American Aﬀordability and Jobs Act
(BAAJA) in September 2026. Key permitting proposals include:

Speeding up approvals for energy projects
Providing greater certainty and consistency across
administrations
Making it easier to permit power transmission lines
Despite debate over its environmental impact, there is broad
agreement that the BAAJA would meaningfully improve the
permitting process. The key question is whether it can become law
under the current Congress. The Senate is expected to vote on 9
November, after which the bill would still need approval from the

House and President Trump. Democrats may want to delay the bill
and seek changes once they regain control of Congress next year,
but doing so could increase the risk of a presidential veto. This
creates an incentive to reach a deal sooner rather than later.

Even if the BAAJA does not pass under the current Congress,
permitting reform is likely to remain a bipartisan theme. The
growing need to expand power supply and infrastructure more
quickly will keep pressure on future Congresses to streamline
permitting.
Business implications
For energy companies – both renewables and fossil fuels –
the BAAJA could signiﬁcantly shorten permitting timelines. If
enacted, it would give project developers greater certainty,

reducing the risk of litigation and permit cancellations. This
could further expand project siting options for developers.
Nevertheless, in the very near term, projects may need to
retain ﬂexibility around their permitting strategy while the
new policy framework is developed and implemented.
Expect more protectionism and reshoring
efforts against China dominance
From clean energy technologies to the critical minerals that
underpin much of the US economy, China controls a large share of

global production capacity. Consequently, reducing reliance on
China will remain a US policy priority. Such eﬀorts would increase
rather than reduce project costs in the near term. But the long-term
beneﬁts of stronger domestic manufacturing and more secure
supply chains could outweigh those costs.

across various sectors

This creates two key risks. First, heavy reliance on China leaves US
industries vulnerable to geopolitical tensions. We have already seen
this with rare earths, where Chinese export restrictions contributed
to disruptions for US auto manufacturers. Second, China’s supply
chain dominance can put US manufacturers at a competitive
disadvantage.
Building more secure energy and natural resource supply chains

has long attracted bipartisan support in the US. The Trump
administration has supported domestic rare earth production
through funding, purchase agreements, and equity investments. In
solar, China-focused trade and tax credit restrictions are also
supporting the expansion of domestic manufacturing.
We expect this focus to continue. The executive branch could
pursue further protective measures and is unlikely to face major
opposition from Congress. Congress could also authorise additional

funding to strengthen rare earth supply chains.
Furthermore, a Democratic-controlled Congress could push to
extend clean manufacturing tax credits as part of broader
negotiations with President Trump. Given the administration’s focus
on countering China and expanding US manufacturing, an
extension could emerge as a compromise in a broader legislative
package under an optimistic scenario. All these eﬀorts would also
extend into future administrations.

Business implications
For US companies:
Clean energy companies and other industries that rely on
rare earths, including semiconductors, AI, defence, and

electronics, should have enough policy certainty to build
long-term supplier relationships outside China. In the near
term, however, diversiﬁcation could raise supply chain costs
as alternative sources may be more expensive. Solar could
face particular cost pressures, as some tariﬀs on supply
chain equipment also apply to non-Chinese suppliers.
For overseas companies:
US eﬀorts to build supply chains outside China are creating
opportunities for manufacturers elsewhere. The US is

supporting rare earth production in Australia and Brazil, while
solar and battery suppliers in countries such as South Korea
and Japan could beneﬁt from stronger demand. This could
also encourage more manufacturing investment in the US.
Meanwhile, European clean energy developers could face
greater competition for non-Chinese supplies. While the EU
has not imposed US-style tariﬀs, it is tightening restrictions
on Chinese equipment through funding, procurement,

auction, and subsidy rules. This could intensify global
competition for non-Chinese suppliers and push costs higher.

Charts of the week: No capitulation

A hotter-than-expected US inflation report triggered a pullback in risk assets, but there are few signs of capitulation so far 
US core and supercore inflation; US inflation breadth*    Our ‘Fear and Greed’ indicator

Source: Bloomberg, Standard Chartered; *share of US consumer inflation components rising more than 3%

Editorial 
Staying defensive and diversified  
Strategy summary: Two weeks ago, we turned defensive 
towards equities, locking in semiconductor equity gains amid 
elevated bond volatility, narrowing market breadth and political 
uncertainty before the US mid-term elections. The near-term 
outlook reflected a less favourable risk-reward balance. Since 
we turned defensive, the Nasdaq 100 index has recorded its 
biggest weekly decline in three months as September’s hotter-
than-expected US inflation report briefly lifted bond yields. 
We have extended our defensive stance this week, 
downgrading US Communication Services equities to Neutral 
and upgrading US Healthcare to Overweight. We also closed 
our opportunistic bullish Communication Services idea and 
initiated a bullish idea on Healthcare, where earnings visibility 
and defensive characteristics appear more attractive. In fixed 
income, we see opportunities in US 10-year government bonds. 
We await clearer AI profit margins and spending guidance, 
broader earnings growth or lower bond and equity market 
volatility before turning positive on equities. Until then, 
diversification and disciplined entry levels remain important. 
Hot US inflation triggers equity pullback; not hot enough 
for an October Fed hike: Risk assets pulled back after 
September’s hotter-than-expected inflation data (headline 3.7% 
y/y; core 2.6% y/y). Higher gasoline and diesel prices were the 
main drivers, but passthrough appeared in airline fares, used 
cars and transportation services, showing pressures were not 
confined to energy. Almost half of inflation components are still 
rising above 3%, a measure recently cited by Fed Chair Warsh. 
Producer inflation also beat estimates. The reports revive the 
possibility of an October Fed rate hike, but we expect a divided 
Fed to weigh it against September’s soft jobs report and 
narrowly hold this month. A December hike appears more likely 
if inflation remains firm. Although Fed Chair Warsh did not 
comment on policy at an IMF speech this week, New York Fed 
chief Williams said he saw no need to hike in 2027. Our base 
case remains two further hikes by June 2027. 
Limited equity pullback so far. Equities fell sharply after the 
US inflation report, but the pullback is limited. The Nasdaq 100 
is 4.5% below its record high, while the S&P 500 remains close 
to all-time highs despite the latest decline. The S&P 500 
volatility index (VIX) has risen from below 15 to 18.2, still well 
under the 25–30 range typically associated with capitulation 
and forced selling. Meanwhile, US government bond volatility 
(MOVE) remains elevated after surging since September. Our 
stock-bond quant model turned more negative (-2) this week, 
signalling elevated equity volatility in the near term.  
When to turn more constructive on stocks? We would turn 
more constructive when capitulation signals align: the Nasdaq 
100 approaches a 10% drawdown, the VIX rises to 25–30 and 
our ‘Fear and Greed’ index enters “extreme fear”. Greater 
confidence would also require easing concerns about AI 
spending and technology firms’ exposure to neocloud revenue, 
lower bond volatility and our stock-bond model turning positive 
again. Until these conditions emerge, volatility may remain 
elevated, favouring a defensive equity stance. Political risk 
should ease after the US mid-term elections. We continue to 
seek selective opportunities following excessive corrections. 
US financials remain a preferred sector after Q3 earnings: 
US large banks’ earnings were mixed, but underlying trends 
remain encouraging. Healthy loan and deposit growth supports 
net interest income, while resilient consumer spending, wealth 
management activity and a solid M&A and refinancing pipeline 
strengthen the outlook. Credit risks appear manageable. We 
remain constructive and favour stronger, diversified franchises. 
US government bonds offer value as yields spike: The 
reversal in US government bond yields after their initial inflation-
driven spike suggests limited further upside. The 10-year yield 
briefly reached 5.32% before slumping as falling equities 
prompted short-covering. We expect a near-term 5.00–5.25% 
range and view moves above 5.25% as attractive entry points 
for our opportunistic 10-year government bond idea. Structural 
pressures, including term premium and increasing supply to 
fund fiscal deficits and AI investments, prevent us from turning 
outright bullish on longer maturities. We, therefore, continue to 
prefer 3–7-year maturities in broader bond allocations.  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya and Sundeep Gantori

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 16 October 2026

Top client questions 
  How does the September 2026 US Consumer Price Index 
(CPI) report alter your view on the USD and bond yields?   
Our view: The hotter-than-expected CPI print raises the odds of 
an October Fed hike, though we still expect a hold followed by 
a rate hike in December. In the near term, we maintain our view 
that the US 10-year government bond yield will range between 
5.00% and 5.25%, while the USD will remain supported as 
markets reassess the likelihood of further Fed hikes. Additional 
gains would depend partly on markets pricing a faster or more 
prolonged tightening cycle, while clearer signs of an 
approaching policy rate peak could see the USD ease back. 
Rationale: September US consumer inflation came in above 
consensus across the board – headline CPI rose 3.7% y/y (vs. 3.6% 
estimate), and core CPI rose 2.6% y/y (vs. 2.5% estimate) – marking 
the fastest headline acceleration in five months. This shifts the 
balance of risks modestly hawkish. Unsurprisingly, the market-
implied probability of an October Fed hike rose over 50% overnight 
but closed the day at 32.7% – 9ppt higher than Tuesday’s close. 
We believe a split Fed will weigh this inflation report against the soft 
September jobs report and narrowly vote to keep rates on hold this 
month. Our base case remains for two more Fed hikes by June 
2027. A December rate hike now looks more like a done deal, 
especially as persistently higher oil prices keep headline and core 
inflation elevated for at least the next few months. Any re-escalation 
of the Middle East conflict after the US mid-term elections would 
exert pressure on the Fed, although we believe Republicans would 
show greater restraint in US foreign action if they suffer steep Senate 
losses, which looks increasingly likely. 
The intra-day price action in US government bonds was instructive. 
The US 10-year yield briefly spiked to 5.32% on the print before 
closing materially lower, as pre-existing bearish positioning was 
unwound and Fed speakers (Barkin, Goolsbee) stopped well short 
of signalling an imminent move. This dynamic reinforces our view 
that the technical selling pressure driving yields towards the upper 
end of our range is fading. Structural drivers – term premium, fiscal 
supply concerns and ‘higher for longer’ Fed repricing – remain intact 
and keep us from turning outright bullish on duration. However, our 
opportunistic bullish idea in US 10-year government bonds initiated 
at September-end remains, as we continue to view spikes in US 10-
year yields over 5.25% as a good risk-reward entry. From a longer-
term portfolio standpoint, we continue to favour the 3-7-year tenor. 
On FX, elevated US government bond yields should sustain the 
USD’s interest rate appeal. However, expected hikes alone will not 
drive further appreciation. Stronger data or firmer Fed guidance 
would provide fresh support, while evidence that tightening is 
nearing completion would temper the USD’s strength. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
— Ray Heung, Senior Investment Strategist

### Page 5

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 16 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What are your key takeaways from US bank earnings? 
Our view: Q3 2026 earnings results from major US banks have 
been mixed, but underlying trends remain encouraging. Healthy 
loan and deposit growth, resilient consumer spending and 
wealth management activity continue to support the sector. 
Credit risks remain manageable. We remain constructive on the 
US financial sector, favouring stronger, diversified franchises. 
Rationale: Healthy loan and deposit growth continue to support net 
interest income (NII), hinting that the US economy remains resilient. 
Capital markets revenue grew year-on-year (y/y), but moderated 
sequentially after a very strong Q2 2026, although performance 
varied across banks. Management commentary points to a healthy 
deal pipeline, supported by M&A activity and debt refinancing 
demand, although execution will depend on market conditions. 
Provisioning and net charge-off trends were mixed across banks, 
with no clear signs of broad deterioration in credit quality. Firmer 
US inflation raises the risk of further monetary tightening. While 
higher rates can support asset yields, they can also increase 
funding costs, pressure borrowers and weigh on securities’ 
valuations. We will continue to monitor lending demand, credit 
costs, NII trends, capital markets momentum and capital returns.  
—  Robin Xie, Senior Investment Strategist 
  Can Big Tech’s earnings guidance sustain confidence in 
the AI investment cycle? 
Our view: We expect Big Tech’s AI spending to remain elevated, 
supporting our constructive AI capex outlook. However, we 
await guidance on the pace of capex growth and the 
sustainability of profit margins. 
Rationale: Semiconductor leaders’ latest company guidance points 
to supply constraints extending through 2028, supporting continued 
capacity expansion for compute. However, while AI capex is still 
likely to rise, y/y growth is expected to moderate from c.100% in 
Q3 2026 to c.20% in Q3 2027. This normalisation in growth could 
raise concerns about the AI investment cycle’s durability.  
The Nasdaq-100 Index has rallied alongside accelerating AI capex. 
Slower capex growth is likely to moderate semiconductor revenue 
growth, while hyperscalers continue to incur depreciation costs on 
past investments. The slower growth is already partially reflected in 
the Nasdaq-100’s valuation de-rating this year. While we remain 
constructive on the AI investment theme, we prefer to stay 
prudent in the near term and await Big Tech’s guidance for greater 
visibility into spending plans in H2 2027 and beyond. 
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist
